- Add opt-in, isolated Preview funding fixtures with one cent of initial synthetic credit. Reseeding never drains or replenishes existing balances, starts work, enables top-ups, or creates Stripe payment evidence.

- Preserve valid Harness approval waits when persistence reorders JSON object keys. Approval identities, tool names, argument values, and array order must still match before any saved decision is consumed.

- Preserve the latest answer and recent tool activity when long assistant transcripts reach storage limits. Repeated compaction keeps one omission notice instead of replacing the final answer with it; both native and personal message schemas accept its metadata, and existing size limits remain enforced.

- Keep Harness control tools available while an answer-only milestone is unfinished, so agents can pause for missing human input instead of treating the plan as already complete. External action tools remain unavailable until the plan requires them.

- Add versioned long-running Harness execution with workspace-funded dispatch holds, renewable execution leases, typed funding pauses, and protected continuation handoff. New checkpoints use gzip-compressed Convex File Storage blobs referenced by an atomic small metadata transaction; older inline checkpoint fields remain readable. Failed/retried uploads may leave unreferenced blobs, and no destructive orphan cleanup is performed. An expired explicit task deadline now persists as nonterminal `waiting_for_authorization`, preserves existing waits, blocks ordinary replies and execution, and remains cancellable. Funding projections show required and available amounts and show reserved amounts only when authoritative. Existing explicit spend overrides, V1 resource pauses, legacy policies, payer distinctions, and unresolved-effect holds remain enforced; targeted fixtures do not qualify every wait handoff or real-time multiweek operation.
- Settle measured model usage when background memory generation fails schema validation, without saving invalid output or leaving a known-cost hold stuck. Unknown usage and missing pricing still require reconciliation.

- Retain receipt-backed provider expenses for failed calls after their wallet holds are released, without charging or crediting the wallet again; include those expenses in billing cost reports.

- Record standing approval for task-related browser QA and testing, and document secure reuse of saved Convex administration access so preview checks do not require repeated approval or per-PR key requests.

- Require paid, funded workspace admission before chat and hosted execution, remove free-model fallback and retired free-routing schema definitions, and preserve manual drafts plus billing-disabled self-hosted access.

- Recover expected Harness milestone-validation failures across durable workflow steps and guide reporting-only milestones to finish from saved results without repeating completed actions.

- Keep provider tool-call markup out of Harness answers and continue to a real final response without repeating completed actions.

- Return workspace-scoped links from note tools so completed tasks can open the saved note without inventing an address.

- Resolve note and Computer download links against successful tool results when a response invents a different hostname.

- Return authenticated download links after Computer file publication and make them clickable even when a response formats the path as text or inline code.

- Configure provider usage markup with `OVERLAY_USAGE_MARKUP_BPS` across web, memory and Computer billing, rejecting invalid settings instead of hiding a fixed rate in code. Subscription prices, included credit and existing subscription records stay unchanged.

- Require a plain-language changelog entry in every PR, including documentation, dependency, test and CI changes, and a functionality summary with deployment status after each merge.

- Reach the first model call in personal Chat and Work runs with 8 durable steps instead of 15. The initial read and execution claim share one step; delivery attach, reauthorization and request accounting share another. Model preparation reuses the state read at the model boundary and reads catalog limits once per model, so later model calls take 4 steps instead of 7. Every authorization, budget and reservation check still runs.
- Explain AI SDK Harness failures in plain language, including provider HTTP failures, invalid inputs and tool approvals, missing outputs, and exhausted SDK retries. Preserve partial-stream warnings and nested SDK causes without exposing prompts, tool inputs, or provider payloads; retry, fallback, and billing behavior are unchanged.

- Prepare paid workspaces for paid-first execution with verified invoice funding and an operator-only, dry-run-first migration that preserves existing balances. Execution access and free-tier infrastructure are unchanged in this preparation release.

- Clear the room and native-agent composer after an acknowledged send, including submitted attachments, without erasing newer drafts or drafts in a reopened conversation.
- Preserve bounded, credential-redacted Harness error messages and causes across serialized and cross-realm exceptions, without changing retry or termination behavior.
- Report provider content-filter rejections hidden inside serialized SDK validation errors explicitly, instead of presenting them as generic Harness failures.
- Retain document filename chips when reloading native-agent and room conversation history.

- Place Organization directly below Timeline in both Work scopes and use one editable header avatar in Organization agent details.

- Separate personal-chief appearance identity from workspace-scoped executable bindings, provisioning distinct private and workspace-scope bindings per eligible owner, with conservative legacy adoption, atomic mixed appearance/config saves, and owner-only profile updates.
- Pin workspace and personal chiefs in the threaded Agents sidebar, preserve owner labels through customization, and add read-only Work → Organization views without changing permissions or introducing delegation.
- Allow the selected agent's thread list to stay collapsed until a different agent is selected, rather than reopening it on every render.
- Include the scoped Organization link in the mobile Work drawer and close the drawer when navigating to it.

- Redesign the operator console to match the app: bordered panels, grouped sidebar (Growth; People and Audit log; Roles, Groups and Catalog), light and dark themes. Growth gains a weekly-active chart, shaded retention and a 4/12/26-week range. People shows each person's name, email, user ID, sign-up date, last active day and plan, with search by name, email or ID. Fixes package styles missing from the admin build.

- Stop sending usage events to PostHog now that growth is counted from Convex. Removed: the `overlay.metrics.*` client and server metrics (BFF requests, model tokens, cache, chat-open latency, duplicate requests, run recovery, session refresh), `retention.engaged`, `auth.session.signed_in`, chat, Work, integration, auth-callback and sign-out events, and the billing, automation and workspace lifecycle mappings. PostHog keeps pageviews, identify/reset, feature flags and one signup event, renamed from `activation.completed` to `user.signed_up`. The unused Convex `withMetrics` wrapper is gone; its table stays until the cleanup cron empties it.

- Add a Growth section to the admin console for admin, auditor and billing operators: weekly active users, new users, messages and paid subscriptions against the prior week; weekly trends for what was created, work done and usage; W1/W4 retention by signup week; and the top workspaces by spend. It reads only the nightly growth rollups.

- Keep agent-created notes, text files and Computer artifacts out of human activity counts while retaining file totals.

- Add nightly growth rollups in Convex: daily counts of what was created and done, active users, W1/W4 retention cohorts, paid subscriptions, top-ups and billed usage, with a one-time backfill. Each file text change is now recorded per day with every person who made it.

- Use saved OAuth sessions when testing MCP connections and keep the MCP editor tabs sized to their labels.

- Standardize MCP and Skills cards and editors on shared shadcn components with flat layouts, accessible Active switches, explicit Manage actions, and deletion confirmation.

- Preserve OAuth MCP authentication, start assigned-agent tasks through the existing collaboration thread and agent-turn pipeline with stable identity and cautious failure reporting, and place contextual/docked resize handles in their gutters.

- Add desktop dictation microphone selection with live device refresh and capture status, alongside native PCM capture without a network admission wait, a compact audio-reactive pill, cancellation and temporary retry/copy recovery.

- Keep the original human reply buffered across opt-in versioned root input, approval, and stuck-wait deadline renewals; production resource admission remains inactive.

- Add an opt-in versioned Harness resource waiter with polling fallback and authoritative consumption at settled response/summary boundaries, including reattachment to existing requests. Production admission remains inactive.

- Allow fully settled Harness results to finish after time or spending authorization is exhausted, without authorizing new work or clearing blockers, child work, or unresolved model holds.

- Add an authenticated personal-owner resource-grant API/client contract and atomic `waiting_for_resources` AgentRun projection. Resource admission remains inactive; workflow polling, wake recovery, and card integration are still open.

- Recheck current room participation and workspace scope before resuming an approval, including requests from the room creator. Revoked access and cross-workspace callbacks cannot wake the task.

- Reconcile missing OpenRouter terminal costs through exact-generation lookup without repeating inference, only under a live zero-priced route lease. Invalid or contradictory metadata, nonzero charges, BYOK, expired leases and cancellation remain fail-closed; late storage acknowledgements cannot authorize settlement.

- Require verified zero-cost router receipts before settling native free-model usage. Missing, unsafe or contradictory provider costs keep the model reservation unresolved instead of being priced as free from the catalog.

- Preserve unresolved free-model summary reservations instead of fabricating a zero-cost failure receipt. Summary fallback and retry require existing settled usage or a confirmed rejection; unknown provider outcomes remain held for reconciliation.

- Keep internal Harness checkpoint summaries out of final assistant replies. Delivery retains explicit results and actual assistant output instead of presenting continuity notes as a user-facing answer after an interruption.

- Recover confirmed free-route unavailability through durable Harness backoff instead of immediately failing unfinished work. Only typed no-inference outcomes can release matching zero-cost holds; ambiguous inference, quarantined routes, paid/BYOK reservations, and authorization limits remain protected.

- Add Harness-scoped binary Computer publication with immutable artifact identity, create-only scoped upload authority, streamed SHA-256 verification, and atomic Files registration. The owner validated the launch-video creation and Overlay Files delivery gate on the PR using their own account; fault-injection and multi-day qualification remain follow-up work.

- Correct binary publication to use the configured FileService, authorize the tool caller before registry or Computer access, refresh grants before snapshot/signing/commit, and treat cleanup after commit as best effort. Enforce the first-version 100 MiB ceiling (or the lower configured direct-upload limit), reject mismatched MIME and dangling folder ancestry, and return safe retry guidance. The owner-accepted video gate covers delivery; replacement-Computer and interrupted-transfer qualification remain follow-up work.

- Preserve declared byte length and requested short lifetime when creating scoped upload URLs through the configured storage provider, with optional validated SHA-256 binding for binary publication.

- Register presigned uploads, consume their intent and account for storage atomically. Repeated publication returns the existing file, while lost acknowledgements and refused registrations preserve bytes for reconciliation instead of deleting potentially published objects.

- Retry settled empty/error context summaries with separate durable backoff and receipt accounting, without resetting response recovery or releasing unresolved model usage.

- Show persisted native-agent failure reasons in personal and room transcripts, preserve unfinished prose, and stop labeling unknown historical failures as connection loss.

- Persist settled Harness response failures with bounded durable retries and receipt accounting, while preserving the existing Harness revision and requiring the additive server-owned `settled-retry-v1` admission marker for new web code.

- Initial long-running harness recovery slice: separate context-compaction checkpoints from continuation stagnation, provide trusted bounded recovery before durable stuck waits, and return repairable generated-UI and plan-validation feedback.

- Cut over personal HTTP and named-room admission to native Harness with explicit retirement responses for unsupported execution paths and truthful fail-closed backend readiness.

- Route automation run-backed and scheduler-owned turns through native Harness with owner-fenced settlement/replay, refreshed bound-agent grants, and approval-denial handling; surface migration and broader legacy retirement remain open.

- Reject new native admissions that attempt to use or adopt retired runners, fence retired-run attachment and transitions, and give owners new-thread guidance instead of resuming legacy approval/input waits while preserving connected remote-agent behavior.

- Fence active retired-run transcript writes, retire unowned room generation, and persist startup failures atomically as terminal errors while preserving native streaming, connected remote writes, and late tool receipts.

- Fence new writes and admissions for conversations or roots with recorded retired native runs, expose authorized read-only compatibility metadata while keeping history readable, and return a truthful 410 retirement response. Untracked historical transcripts, deployed-worker drain, and the new-thread UI remain open.

- Keep tracked-thread safety compatible with web-first rollout: older backends preserve transcript reads with explicit unavailable metadata and block new thread writes/admissions with a retriable 503 until the server-owned backend marker is present. The new backend remains authoritative for 410 retirement classification; already-admitted remote execution remains unchanged.

- Show read-only footers for retired or temporarily unavailable native threads, gate personal and room mutations on fresh safety checks, preserve drafts after compatibility rejections, provide editable bounded handoffs to fresh threads, and keep compatibility GET failures from triggering recheck loops.

- Keep Generated UI edits unavailable without a persistence callback, explicitly pass retired-thread safety through personal and room transcripts, and discard local edits when a card becomes read-only.

- Keep a blank personal-chat handoff synchronized with the Next.js URL state when removing the prior conversation ID.

- Preserve normally typed composer text when native-thread safety suspends and remounts the editor.

- Preserve completed tool results when recovering a failed Harness model stream without carrying forward partial failed-call output.

- Add an opt-in Preview-only historical-thread retirement fixture for hosted UI QA without scheduling workflows or replenishing existing QA allowance. Preview builds can seed it through the trusted controller without exporting database credentials.

- Switch existing accounts to double sidebar once across all workspaces, while allowing subsequent single-sidebar opt-outs.

- Keep the gray gutters equal on both sides of onboarding while its navigation is hidden.

- Add the curved double-sidebar layout: a persistent icon rail, independently collapsible contextual panels, matching rounded borders around navigation and detail panels, and indented agent threads. Align the composer with the full message stream. New layout preferences default to double sidebar; saved single-sidebar choices remain unchanged.

- Right-size Namespace CI runners, prioritize required checks, preserve exact dependency caching with an opt-in protected local-volume experiment, and add project-aware pre-push typechecking and opt-in compiler/lint/test-selection parity tools.

- Render durable personal-run input requests, persist authenticated approval-resumed tool outcomes, and use one plain-language interruption notice without duplicate copy.
- Preserve the visible first-turn transcript when Stop activates the saved conversation during backend cancellation.

- Unify personal Chat and Work into one durable Chat experience with backend-capability-selected execution, while preserving approval, billing, and usage-limit checks.

- Fix chat branching silently losing copied messages, use GLM 5.3 Flash for budget-authorized titles with UTF-8-safe input holds and a local fallback, preserve prose, code and standalone display equations around LaTeX without leaking partial-stream placeholders, and label workspace AI conversations "Chats".

- Use TinyFish as the primary web search provider and power deep search with bounded multi-query Search + Fetch, with Elo fallback for both tools.

- Reserve model budgets against Gateway provider, regional, fast and peak prices rather than only headline rates, preventing underfunded chat and context-summary settlements when routing selects a more expensive provider.

- Release Computer wake claims when provider reconnection fails, and safely reconcile expired maintenance before retrying without replacing the disk or bypassing usage settlement.

- Keep desktop reply notifications in their original workspace and wait for saved chat IDs.

- Keep desktop dictation and screenshots scoped to their original draft; release cancelled microphones and refresh native status live.

- Decode desktop capture to PCM for offline models and reject stale recordings after cancellation.

- Default desktop dictation to Control+Space and add a test-notification action with macOS permission guidance.

- Fix native dictation capture startup and exercise the actual recorder HUD and signed clean/upgrade profiles in macOS CI.

- Stop native desktop work and close floating panels when signing out from the sidebar.

- Normalize empty evidence-tool lists for answer-only agent milestones so simple replies can finish; action milestones still require execution evidence.

- Allow credit-funded cloud dictation when no legacy seconds cap is configured, while retaining paid-plan, explicit quota and budget reservation checks.

- Keep desktop floating panels compact after chat navigation and reload by using native window identity.

- Keep agent model routing aligned with the admitted model after a free-plan or budget fallback, so an agent turn can start instead of failing before inference.

- Record desktop shortcuts directly from key presses and expose per-device crash-reporting consent when available; bound native sign-out cleanup so web logout stays responsive.

- Add the versioned macOS desktop bridge, native settings/composer controls and compact chat/notes panels while sharing the full web app and session. Retire the duplicate desktop application and preserve local upgrade data.

- Use text-only tool, reasoning, worked-for and approval rows, with one 24px look-motion mark below active assistant output; only its eyes move.

- Replace Overlay web branding and static assets with the O-with-eyes mark, capitalized lockup, eye-motion loaders and record-backed, read-only default-agent avatar.

- Keep Chat and Work available during the Harness web-first rollout by checking backend readiness before admission; recognize already-terminal workflows during Stop cleanup.

- Preserve native Harness approval waits beyond the legacy room lease; only the durable run deadline ends those waits.

- Allow the initiating user to answer native Harness input waits after rechecking current room participation.

- Recheck personal Harness tool approvals at execution and require the authenticated controller decision before an approval-required effect.

- Settle model usage before durable approval waits so Approve and Deny can resume without reusing a model reservation or blocking context compaction.

- Show "Backed by verdict." above the landing headline and replace the Hermes agent chip with "+ more".

- Run Linux GitHub Actions jobs on the shared Namespace runner profile.

- Reuse exact CI dependency trees and compiler/lint/type caches, run lint and typechecking independently, and balance exhaustive test shards without weakening required checks.

- Add trusted-main Preview fixture recovery so coding-agent VMs can request bounded quota/credit recovery without receiving a Convex deploy key.

- Repair scheduled workflow event projection through the authenticated web host, with paged, replay-safe Convex storage and catch-up for short completed runs.

- Return Stripe checkout, top-up and portal flows to the workspace that started them, including when another tab changes the active workspace.

- Restrict automation workflow status/events to the parent automation's scope and current workspace membership, and bind scheduler cancellation to its owner's stored workflow ID.

- Queue automation completion/failure webhooks in the same transaction as terminal run settlement, including durable scheduled runs and idempotent retries.

- Restore workspace-scoped webhook delivery history and redrive by accepting the app's workspace context and checking each delivery's parent subscription.

- Remove the separate Browser Use service, API and tools; interactive browsing now uses the bound Computer and its existing admission, approvals and machine billing.

- Add audited Preview-only free-route quarantine recovery that requires fresh provider verification before reuse.

- Run the production cron schedule on fresh previews and cap metered Preview spending across wallets independently of synthetic-credit refills.

- Initialize WorkOS password and retained sessions before onboarding so newly invited users have profile state; add an isolated exhausted-quota QA fixture with explicit recovery.

- Provision isolated Stripe test webhooks for billing-enabled previews, retain their signing secrets across rebuilds, and verify owned endpoint cleanup before deleting a Preview backend.

- Add Preview capability diagnostics, combined paid/Computer fixtures, explicit audited QA recovery, isolated protected callbacks, production schedules with controlled test delivery, and preconfigured owner/member/outsider QA sign-in.

- Abort title generation at its shared deadline or client cancellation, stop retries, and release failed-attempt reservations.

- Keep generated chat titles stable through optimistic updates and use a resolved first-message title when creating chats.
- Add an agent sidebar close button and preserve reply row identity from pending through streaming and completion, preventing header blink.
- Include agent-created automations in the Automations list, show their agent avatars, and open them from agent rows.
- Start new agent conversations with the agent's instructions as the first user message.
- Generate chat titles with the Auto free router and retry stalled title requests once.
- Retry empty Auto title generations, skip free models that return an empty reply, and record routed-model diagnostics.

- Preserve connector-card links for unavailable services and render the Poolside provider mark without opaque export backgrounds.

- Refresh recommended models to each provider's latest releases, give every model provider a logo, show the app's logo on chat connect cards and switch them to Connected once linked, and use the shared model picker in the New agent dialog with GLM 5.3 Flash as the default. Free workspaces now see only Auto and OpenRouter free in model pickers even when billing is turned off for the deployment.
- Avoid remounting new chats after their first response and keep the generation-mode tooltip from opening when the model picker receives programmatic focus.

- Keep free workspaces visibly free until paid, show locked premium models with upgrade paths, and surface paid and weekly usage exhaustion in chat.

- Enable ChatGPT/Codex subscription connections in the hosted production environment. Other subscription adapters remain unavailable.

- Keep the composer available when a disconnected BYOK model is selected, with guidance to reconnect or choose another model.

- Track pending credential-vault writes so orphaned objects are deleted after process crashes; disconnect and account deletion retain tracking until in-flight writes have drained.

- Keep a selected subscription or API-key model on new conversations for free-tier accounts instead of resetting to Auto.

- Strip Codex-incompatible output and sampling controls before forwarding subscription inference requests.

- Fix subscription authorization failing to store sign-in state because the credential-vault context contained an unsupported character.

- Preserve safe failure codes, HTTP statuses, upstream error codes, and offending field names in credential-vault logs so subscription authorization failures can be diagnosed without exposing credentials or upstream response bodies.

- Move Account outside workspace settings, add workspace-specific profiles with provider-photo fallbacks, and let workspace admins choose or upload icons. Retire the outdated interactive demo.

- Keep native agents running after a failed tool-progress write and compact live tool results before saving them to the transcript.

- Retry delayed Gateway usage before settling known token counts from catalog pricing, preserve reservation-integrity errors, and safely cancel named descendants after an ancestor stops.

- Separate tagged model reasoning from Harness final answers and delegated result summaries using the AI SDK middleware, preserving provider usage receipts.

- Join active Harness children with durable waits instead of model polling, and explicitly request the final deliverable once all plan evidence is complete.

- Wait durably for busy free-model routes before inference, retaining exclusive route leases and zero-cost rejection receipts. Convex accepts the same bounded rejection reasons as the runtime. Ambiguous provider failures still retain billing holds and never authorize another Harness dispatch.

- Persist completed child-run results as parent observations so delegated milestones can reference their output without treating it as independent verification. Reading a completed result first settles the parent's child state, avoiding a completion race while retaining unresolved-usage safeguards. Children inherit their parent's planning policy instead of requiring unnecessary plans for trivial replies.

- Fix Harness Workflow initialization by configuring model retry limits on compiler-safe step functions, keeping credentials and authorization inside durable execution.

- Recover personal Chat and Work sends after a pre-dispatch session rejection, using the shared browser session refresh and preserving turn identity without replaying provider failures.

- Add the shared Overlay Harness execution and persistence foundations: evidence-gated plans, durable state operations, semantic continuation, secure model references and single-workflow ownership. Authenticated personal Chat and Work now use these foundations; other surfaces await migration and parity QA.

- Add workflow-owned input/approval wait storage, child attention notifications and an owner-only personal run input API for the upcoming harness migration. Personal tool approvals also remain owner-only in shared conversations.

- Route authenticated personal Chat and Work turns through durable Harness Workflow admission with a default $1 Overlay model-cost cap and 24-hour deadline, configurable up to $100 and seven days. New personal AgentRuns carry the immutable `executionEngine: 'overlay_harness'` discriminator; legacy rows and same-turn replays remain unmarked and owned by their existing runners. In-flight runs and internal automation remain on legacy runners; UI work, other surfaces and hosted parity QA are pending.

- Bind a personal delivery AgentRun to its root harness atomically at admission and fence the root atomically when Stop cancels that delivery. Workflow SDK cancellation is best-effort physical cleanup after the durable fence; it does not promise to abort provider work already dispatched. Final result summaries remain separately persisted from working summaries.

- Add bounded, freshly authorized model fallback to the personal harness after confirmed pre-inference rejections. Ambiguous provider failures keep their unresolved spend holds and do not dispatch again. Fallback notices stream and persist with the final transcript for authenticated personal Chat and Work.

- Saved collaborative message edits now update rendered text and retain attachments instead of showing the original message.

- Use shadcn focus-visible rings for text inputs and textareas, present message editing with text actions, autosize work titles and agent instructions as layouts settle, and group agent Details into collapsed Model, Instructions, Tools and Advanced disclosures.
- Settings breadcrumbs read Settings > page instead of a Personal or Workspace prefix.

- Subscription connections now use request-based Codex endpoints in the Overlay app, with durable Convex coordination and credentials held in the vault; the retired broker is no longer required. Other provider adapters remain unavailable until individually implemented, and subscription inference never falls back to Overlay-paid routing.

- Return not found for a removed workspace retained in an API client's active-workspace header.

- Reject native approval decisions from users outside the conversation with a clear not-found response.

- Retain native-agent tool calls and their real results when durable turns finalize, so completed execution evidence survives reload.

- Clear connection-error notices when retrying a remote agent that produced no text; preserve genuine partial replies and their recovery offsets.

- Reuse explicit Preview-only, non-refilling machine QA funding while keeping ordinary Personal previews free.

- Limit selectable BYO providers to Claude Code and Codex while preserving retired account and
  workspace-agent records for history and removal. Prepare Agent Host/protocol `0.4.3`, Claude
  ACP `0.93.0`, Codex ACP `2.2.2`, and agent image v10; publishing and idle machine upgrades are
  not part of this source change.
- Move agent reconnect controls into the message hover toolbar: Stop cancels connection attempts, Retry appears only after recoverable failure, and inline waiting text and buttons are removed.

- Require server authentication for task scope, archive and restore so browser tokens cannot bypass Work policy or forge agent activity.

- Keep task revisions increasing when a comment follows an edit in the same clock tick, so stale updates remain rejected.

- Preserve native-agent attribution when task tools archive, restore, or move a task.

- Overlay Free no longer cuts off longer OpenRouter Free replies with overlay_free_stream_prelude_too_large.

- Add task tools for listing, reading, creating, updating, commenting on, archiving, and restoring Work tasks, with agent activity attribution. Updates check revisions inside the mutation and commit fields, status and scope together, preserving concurrent edits and avoiding partial changes.
- Chat can now find archived tasks to restore them.
- Let workspace-visible and personally owned agents take Personal tasks, keep creator-only agents private from other members, and preserve assignment eligibility when tasks change scope.

- Restore Resume and Release control for the controlling user after reloading an agent conversation.
- Keep synthetic Computer QA funding behind explicit Preview fixture opt-in while preserving existing test balances.
- Allow native-agent workflow retries to recapture the screen without replaying clicks, typing, commands or unknown tools. Keep fresh authorization and billing checks, and log safe preparation-failure metadata.

- Browser tools now report expired element references as recoverable stale references and direct agents to refresh their snapshot without guessing or clicking a replacement target.
- Apply Stripe webhook event markers and workspace billing updates atomically so failed writes remain retryable without duplicate credit grants.
- Keep agent Computer controls and background refreshes scoped to the current workspace and agent, including wake and takeover responses.
- Workspace Stripe checkout now propagates subscription persistence failures and omits unsupported customer-sync fields. Attaching the first Stripe subscription preserves existing usage, reservations, and top-up credit instead of treating the local allowance as a renewed subscription.
- Harden Computer browser profile lock recovery around stale or reused PIDs while protecting live profile owners.
- Add durable Ask approvals for native agents in direct messages and rooms, with pending calls resuming without replaying prior tool calls. Release QA remains required.
- Add Ask and Auto permission modes for Overlay agents.
- Work approval prompts now appear even if the live update connection stalls.
- Work approval prompts now appear during the first turn of a new chat.
- Require confirmation for risky coordinate clicks on browser controls.
- Fix ordinary clicks in the browser being asked for approval.
- Show Computer control progress only for control actions, not start, stop or delete.
- Alert on delayed sandbox metering and failed stop or cleanup operations.

- Keep agent Details and Computer tabs synchronized after starting, stopping, waking or deleting the attached computer.

- Standardize workspace headers on shadcn breadcrumbs, keep Workspace agent selection separate from remembered Personal threads, simplify agent thread controls, shorten composer prompts, and correct response hover controls and direct-message dialog sizing. Returning through the Files breadcrumb clears the selected editor and ignores stale file loads. Editable note titles retain enabled accessibility semantics.

- Refresh the agent conversation roster after agent creation, edits and archive so newly created agents open without a page reload. Ignore stale requests and unrelated workspace changes.

- Launch with native Overlay agents and their computers while keeping external cloud and own-machine runtimes behind a default-off release gate. Hide unavailable external setup, reject direct creation and execution bypasses, and retain authorized history, cancellation, cleanup and settlement. Add `FEATURES.md` with feature switches, provider support and re-enablement steps.

- Persist tool failures and invalid calls as terminal error states, retain successful tool output across streamed steps, and give native agents exact recovery guidance for the tools exposed in the current turn.

- Keep sidebar data isolated while switching workspaces by publishing the workspace after navigation, resetting sidebar state, and binding chat-list requests to their cache workspace.
- Patch undici v7 and v8 dependency copies to 7.30.1 and 8.12.2 for GHSA-xjj6-f33q-929c without changing supported major versions or security gates.
- Recover expired sessions on protected pages before redirecting to sign-in, preserving the requested workspace page instead of unexpectedly opening Chats.
- Keep workspace navigation on immutable IDs until member-scoped resolution confirms a routable handle; existing accounts remain usable without migration or backfill.
- Add indexed workspace handles with permanent aliases, authenticated resolution, owner-only changes, and a resumable dry-run-capable backfill. Existing workspace IDs and ID-based links remain supported.
- Give scoped workspace features canonical `/w/<handle>/<personal|workspace>/<feature>` addresses, including Work, Agents, Chats, Files, Notes, Outputs, Tools and Automations. Solo workspaces retain both views.
- Keep newly created empty channels joined when navigating away, including through older draft links.
- Preserve the selected workspace and personal/workspace scope when the first chat stream creates a conversation before the parallel metadata request.
- Show the Agents empty state when the selected creator scope has no agents, even if the opposite scope is populated.
- Patch vulnerable Zod v4 installs at 4.6.7, including transitive production dependencies, while preserving existing v3 schemas through explicit compatibility imports.
- Patch production body-parser to 2.4.0 and Mintlify's nested copy to 1.20.9 for GHSA-27r3-jxmh-p828.
- Reveal chat copy actions on hover or keyboard focus, and add an agent-conversation sidebar as a full-height panel with one editable hero, autosaving Details, conversation-linked Library files and an always-visible Computer tab with control mode. Files and notes created by an agent are linked to its conversation and appear in the Library. Room panels and the agent sidebar now share the same right-hand slot. Agent settings use the shared chat model picker with free-tier and zero-data-retention access rules.

- Update `@sentry/nextjs` to 10.75.3 and `dompurify` to 3.4.17 to fix the Sentry tunnel middleware bypass (GHSA-mchm-gjx6-7h55) and two DOMPurify sanitizer advisories (GHSA-cg2g-xh64-v4q7, GHSA-mrv2-3pqr-j5jr) that fail the production dependency audit.

- Make the docs logo and favicon match the web app: the lockup SVGs follow `BrandLockup` (orb 0.5em on the baseline, 0.4em gap, Libre Baskerville Medium at -0.025em, drawn as outlines so no font fallback) at the landing nav's 22px, and the favicon is the app's own icon instead of a full-bleed orb.

- Clean up the Self-hosting docs: a short overview of what you run replaces the 554-line page, whose sections move to new Node.js install, Upgrades and Backups pages and to Environment variables (a where-each-secret-goes table that fixes `INTERNAL_SERVICE_AUTH_SECRET` belonging on both the app and Convex), Providers (transactional email and MCP credential encryption) and Runtime config (example profiles). The removed Postgres app-data mode, LayerNorm's internal deploy scripts and the retired budget API are gone, the admin page now describes the standalone admin console and break-glass first admin, and page titles use sentence case. This completes the docs restructure; its plan is deleted.

- Write the Developer docs: an MCP server page (endpoint, OAuth 2.1 and personal tokens, access levels, approvals, limits), Authentication, and Webhooks (events, payloads, signature verification, retries). The REST API overview is rewritten, the generated reference drops 42 internal routes (onboarding, the Agent Host protocol, UI helpers and the retired subscription settings) via `publicReference: false`, the stale admin guide is removed, and the billing guide's maintainer notes move to `internal/develop/workspace-billing.md`.

- Rewrite the Help docs against the current app: new Get started, Work, Extensions and Plans and credit pages; Chats, Files and notes, Memories, Automations, Settings and FAQ now describe Personal/Workspace scopes, workspace billing, Settings → Memories and Settings → Archived. The Projects page is removed and redirects to Files and notes.

- Retheme the docs to match the landing page: Geist at weight 500 with tight tracking instead of serif headings, neutral `#171717` ink with a `#111111` dark background, mono eyebrows and sidebar group titles, hairline cards and code blocks, a pill "Open app" button and GitHub/Discord footer links. The docs home is now a short page with Help, Self-hosting and Developer cards.

- Split the public docs into Help, Self-hosting and Developer tabs (plus Changelog). Configure, Deploy & Operate and licensing pages move under `docs/self-hosting/`; the API reference, contributor guides and security reporting move under `docs/developer/`, with a new Developer overview. Maintainer-only pages (release and agentic workflow, preview QA, Convex workflow, admin console, API source of truth and route catalogs, customization, feature modules, Overlay Free, docs hosting) move to `internal/develop/`. Every old URL redirects. `docs:health`, the licensing and tenancy checks, AGENTS.md and the READMEs follow the new paths.

- Stop offering the desktop and mobile apps, which are not available. `/download` now redirects to `/home` unless `OVERLAY_DESKTOP_DOWNLOADS_ENABLED=1` (the page and release plumbing stay for later), and it leaves the sitemap and footer. The landing page lists the web, Slack, ChatGPT, Claude, Cursor and MCP apps instead of macOS, Windows, iOS and Android, and its "Get Overlay" card offers Web, your AI apps, self-hosting and developer links. The account page's Desktop app panel and the profile menu's "Soon" app items are removed. The 18 Desktop docs pages are deleted and redirect to the Help Center. Desktop sign-in handoff, mobile auth completion and the account-deletion deep link are unchanged.

- Bring every other marketing page onto the home page design: pricing, download, manifesto, the blog/changelog/self-hosting placeholders and all nine legal pages now share one `StaticMarketingShell` (landing nav, framed column, footer) with a common page head. Legal pages get a sticky table of contents, pricing's FAQ uses the shared accordion, and `/download` regains its nav and footer. The old marketing navbar, button, reveal animation and serif layout helper are removed.
- Let people who left sign-up before verifying finish from sign-in. Signing in with the right password for an unverified account now opens the code screen with Resend, then signs in once the code is accepted, instead of linking to a missing `/auth/verify-email` page. Sign-in and sign-up also show a plain fallback message instead of crashing when an error response is not text.

- Add account-level workspace discovery, freeform workspace creation and invitation entry before onboarding. Suggested answers fill an editable composer in main chat and agent conversations without submitting automatically. New owners get a team question; invited members get a shorter setup. Restore profile drafts on refresh and remove the tour target border.

- Replace the `/home` scroll story with a static landing page in one framed column (approved mockup `internal/plans/mockups/landing-static-mockup-v2.html`): a one-color headline whose five underlined phrases jump to their sections, a still of the app with the live sidebar (Work, Chats, Files, Automations) and agents drawn as their creatures, then superpowers, the create/manage/deploy/bring-your-own steps, teams, controls, where to get Overlay and the FAQ. The footer's Product links now point at `#control-plane`, `#get-overlay`, `#ai-employees` and `#deploy`. The scroll engine, scene graphics and interactive demo are removed.

- Added MiniMax M3.1 Flash (free, vision) to the model list.
- Keep the model you pick in an existing chat after sending. The composer and saved chat model no longer revert to the chat's earlier model once the reply starts.

- Show loading branding immediately, keep onboarding answers in the normal message style and anchor the workspace tour beside navigation. Open one-to-one AI employee conversations in Agents instead of Chats.

- Make the `/home` app still look like the real workspace: the live sidebar (Personal/Workspace, Work, Agents, Chats, Automations, More, Needs you / Assigned to me / Everything), no header rules, an Operations agent planning a team offsite with the orb "Worked for" tool row, and its computer streaming a browser that fills in a venue form. The FAQ now uses a new shadcn `Accordion` in `@overlay/ui/primitives`, and the marketing footer keeps two or four link columns on narrow screens instead of one long column.

- Redesign onboarding as a scripted conversation with no model calls: the same answers always give the same questions, employee suggestions and starter tasks. Controls sit directly under Overlay's question, single-choice chips answer in one tap, and "You can call me Divyansh" is saved as "Divyansh". App and Claude Code/Codex connections share one step with brand logos and are hidden or skipped when unavailable. Remove the website question and personalization consent, phrase the user's replies naturally, hide message actions during setup, and add Skip setup.

- Keep onboarding tour controls inside the mobile navigation drawer, with visible navigation targets and automatic drawer cleanup when the tour ends.

- Make conversational onboarding sessions reset-safe with run-scoped transcript recovery and durable summaries of validated user choices.

- Keep earlier messages visible after a reply finishes in a chat reopened from history. Restored chats now load the full transcript into the active chat runtime instead of only the current mode's turns.

- Remove multi-model selection and compare responses across text, image, and video chats. Existing saved model preferences and conversations continue with one primary model response.

- Run Computer commands with injected environments across compound shell bodies, expose bounded input diagnostics, and ensure browser pages and windows are visible.

- Preserve Work screenshot model output and approval cards, and recover Chromium profile locks.

- Retry concurrent Computer actions, expose personal Computers in Work chats, and clarify busy and approval errors.

- Add human takeover controls for Computers and require approval for risky browser clicks.

- Add persistent, cancellable terminal sessions for Computer commands.

- Require Work-mode approval for typing and key presses on Computers, and fail closed in regular chats.

- Add persistent browser controls for agents, including snapshots and ref-based actions.

- Add provider-neutral desktop screenshots and input controls for Overlay agents.

- Align Work quick-add with the sidebar scope, keep workspace storage quotas visible, and make sidebar create actions permanent with neutral placeholders, toasts, and centered loading marks.
- Announce stopped and failed Work runs only when their status transitions live to a terminal state.
- Add OpenCode Go to Settings > Providers. Chat, Responses, and Messages model protocols use the OpenCode Go subscription endpoint with stable conversation sessions.

- Match file and automation sidebar rows to chats with shared 32px rows, 14px text and 16px icons, reduce agent names to the conversation text size, and give every shared chat composer a subtle theme-aware outline.

- Pin `follow-redirects` to 1.16.1 to clear two high-severity advisories (GHSA-q3jh-wrc6-jgq8, GHSA-8r9p-f939-6h3c) that block the production dependency audit.

- Report unowned Boat and E2B machines from bounded inventory scans without stopping or deleting them.

- Record cloud agent allocations before setup and reclaim machines whose setup was interrupted instead of leaving them running unbilled.

- Correct Computer Start and Stop notifications to match the completed action.

- Add an isolated, synthetic funded Computer QA workspace to Preview fixtures while retaining the free Personal workspace. Re-seeding preserves spent credit, holds and plan changes; no Stripe payment or automatic top-up is created.

- Enroll persistent Computers in the shared sandbox lease ledger before provider allocation or wake. Meter Boat's weighted counters, reserve both the runtime member and agent spend subject, preserve disks on idle/budget stops, and retain durable cleanup and allocation references for retries. Computer deletion settles usage before removing the provider resource; this is a scoped lifecycle/accounting substep, not completion of Phase 2.

- Stop running machines and Computers at the next meter tick after a workspace leaves its paid plan, while still settling confirmed-stopped usage and releasing unused holds.

- Recheck paid workspace and finite-credit admission for cloud-agent machine allocation, explicit resume/restart controls, workflow wakes and stopped-execution recovery under the authenticated actor or the remote run's stored actor. Remote resume, retry and start-fresh are refused before any control is recorded. Pause and cleanup remain available after downgrade.

- Settle E2B compute from completed provider executions and actual allocation, reserve credit while executions run, and retain final usage for retry if billing fails. Enforce workspace and subject limits on machine metering; preserve Boat's weighted counters instead of substituting elapsed time.

- Bind Computer access to the authorized workspace and require paid workspace credit before provisioning, starting, desktop access or tool execution. Concurrent owner claims allocate only one machine; failed deletion retains the provider reference for retry, and lifecycle reads never implicitly resume a machine.

- Make both sidebar edges resizable, keep native agent text visible between tool calls while it streams, preserve its generation order after completion and reload, and match the approved agent hierarchy with regular-weight names, aligned icons and flush-left threads. Scale tool Orbs to the wordmark ratio and center them beside wrapped labels. Use the shared avatar popover for outside-click and Escape dismissal.

- Preserve the live transcript when a new chat receives its saved conversation ID after completion, while still resetting scroll state when navigating between chats.

- Widen the model picker, separate personal Favorites from Recommended, expose the full provider catalog, and move capabilities to right-side details. Free accounts can browse the same catalog metadata without gaining permission to generate with paid models. Remove the catalog enable/disable settings while retaining provider-key authorization and billing/ZDR safeguards. Keep streamed Markdown on the same block renderer through chat and agent completion without a completion label or automatic work collapse moving the live answer.

- Stop the app half-loading after bursts of throttled reads: Work list, Work context, subscription and workspace policy reads get their own per-user rate-limit buckets instead of sharing the default pool. Workspace bootstrap and policy requests now recover an expired session like other app requests. App pages without their own error boundary keep the shell, and both error screens show the error reference.

- Standardize shared controls on the approved gray palette, align Work rows and headers, and move transient action feedback to bottom-right Sonner notifications. Keep upload updates under stable operation ids and preserve Undo, inline validation, and durable recovery states.

- Recover browser API requests from an expired session without a page reload: share one locked session refresh with the auth provider, then retry once with the original mutation idempotency key. Signed-out users, permission denials, and failed refreshes remain failures.

- Match the app shell mockup: Geist as the app font, 14px chat text, a borderless symmetric user bubble, and sidebar rows at 14px with 16px icons and 12px section titles. Native `title` tooltips are replaced app-wide by one shadcn-style tooltip (`NativeTitleTooltips`).

- Adopt shadcn Typeset (`@overlay/ui/typeset.css`, an owned copy) as the shared prose layer for chat messages and notes, so rendered Markdown gets one set of rules for paragraphs, lists, quotes, code, tables and rules. Existing chat-specific Markdown rules still take precedence.

- Replace hand-styled buttons that copied the solid, gray or outlined look with the shared Button variants across chat, settings, sharing, notes and files, and extend `check:raw-controls` to fail on `unstyledButton()` strings that rebuild a variant or color classes passed to a non-ghost Button (exceptions carry an `overlay:button-style:` reason).

- Give each page one solid create action (New skill, Add server, New note) and switch other hand-styled header, dialog and empty-state buttons on Skills, MCPs, Connectors, Notes and Automations to the shared `primary` and `secondary` Button variants.

- Alias the shadcn design tokens (`--primary`, `--secondary`, `--muted-foreground`, `--destructive`, `--card`, `--popover`, `--input`, `--ring`, `--radius`) to the Overlay palette, and add `outline` and `link` Button variants plus `xs`, `icon-sm` and `icon-xs` sizes. No visual change.

- Make a single shadcn sidebar the default app shell: Personal/Workspace toggle under the brand, Files and Extensions under a hover More menu, page subviews as tabs on the header row, and no divider lines between sidebar, header and body. The rail and panel layout remains under Settings > Customization. Add Workspace AI chats: chats started from Workspace > Chats are filed under that scope (stored as an optional conversation `scope`; existing chats stay Personal). The Personal/Workspace toggle always shows; in a one-person workspace Workspace is disabled with a hint to invite members. Settings swaps the sidebar for a settings sidebar: Back to app, a settings search (matches page names and the settings on each page), then the settings pages in groups. The onboarding tour, its Replay tour setting, and the first-run workspace naming step are removed. Work's Needs you, Assigned to me and Everything filters move from chips under the header into collapsible sidebar sections that list their open tasks. Page headers read title and count, then search, actions, tabs, and the options menu last, so page tabs sit at the far right. The agent conversation's Share action moves into its options menu.

- Keep the onboarding tour's first tooltip anchored next to the model picker, and render the "Connect your tools" logos inline so they appear on first paint.
- Standardize the orb + wordmark on `BrandLockup` across the loader, landing, auth, and marketing surfaces, and play the loader's word reveal only once so it keeps blinking until the app appears. Once the app has rendered real content the loader can never reappear: boundary remounts and route fallbacks show plain skeletons instead.
- Make first-run onboarding deterministic: it starts only on the post-signup redirect or an explicit replay, waits for the workspace to load before the name step, mounts the tour only on the chat surface where its targets render, holds the model picker open through the tour's early steps, and no longer refires on refresh or pops up mid-session.

- Fix connected-agent Drive recovery after remote edits, release leases after confirmed startup failure, and retain fencing for uncertain writers. Authorize shared-room run status correctly, bound repeated Drive reads, and derive QA adapter defaults from image pins.

- Make caught agent failures traceable across response, server logs and Sentry with diagnostic ids, preserved backend request ids, safe cause signatures and bounded source locations. Keep unknown causes explicit and raw exception content private.
- Keep connected-agent output streaming during uploads with coalesced text, byte-bounded batches, durable retry boundaries and negotiated incremental text. Preserve partial answers and tool history through cancellation, failure and reconnects.
- Render live replies as formatted Markdown with bottom loading dots, compact tool groups, accurate tool ages and task deadlines. Preserve literal identifiers and wrap long hashes in tables.
- Discover model, effort and permission choices from the bound agent account before the first task; persist and apply the selected settings across native recovery.
- Add execution-fenced workspace-tool calls and resumable approval delivery with one decision card, signed expiry and current-access checks. Show approval expiry and ongoing machine costs.
- Add guarded Drive synchronization with exact revisions, immutable upload receipts, full-byte verification, bounded hydration and conflict preservation. Save incrementally and wait for final file settlement before reporting completion.
- Open saved agent deliverables in Files by verified file ids, including relative links in answers and scoped sibling/descendant links inside Markdown deliverables, and load text uploads with their complete stored content.
- Add opt-in same-conversation recovery for lifecycle-v2 Claude Code and Codex hosts. Fence replacement execution, reconcile interrupted writes before hydration, preserve selected settings and reject uncertain starts or unresolved approvals.
- Confirm native termination before releasing ownership; drain buffered output and active writes during shutdown. Keep Stop available during startup and final saving, and settle interrupted action timers after recovery.
- Report enrollment failures and connection limits accurately, retain account setup after machine deletion, and keep fresh-machine hydration authorized without claiming provider liveness prematurely.
- Reduce repeated authorization and billing reads on agent uploads while retaining current access, wallet, revocation and legacy-backend checks. Read Drive sources with bounded parallelism and deterministic cache limits.
- Add request ids, safe failure classifications and per-run stream counters to connected-agent diagnostics. Extend the cloud smoke to run through Overlay, and add opt-in Preview timing exports with bounded storage and visible capacity.
- Harden oversized tool titles, Unicode limits, untrusted text parsing and storage download boundaries. Keep protected Preview access in host-only state and retain hosted TypeScript validation in memory-optimized builds.
- Keep Turbopack route bootstraps external so the optimized app hydrates under nonce-based Content Security Policy after the Next.js upgrade.

- Preserve protected-route query parameters through sign-in and stale-session reauthentication so desktop login handoffs retain their PKCE challenge.

- Patch Next.js to 16.4.1 in both web apps and Better Auth/SSO to 1.7.7. Replace the privileged PR preview trigger with a secret-free PR request that dispatches the protected main controller.
- Retry cloud cancellation stop-proof checks from the durable coordinator timer after the notification has been consumed, preventing stuck cancelling runs and retained holds.

- Scope Boat Preview machine creation to its isolated named environment while keeping account credentials excluded; document the approved one-year Preview key renewal requirement.

- Preserve connected-agent approval/input waits across transcript batches and healthy long waits. Add opt-in versioned remote lifecycle coordination, fenced host observations, durable control/finalization intents, truthful recovery limits, and reconnect output protection. Host/protocol candidate: 0.3.11; image candidate: v6. New enrollment remains disabled pending hosted/provider acceptance.

- Complete operator backend separation: retire customer-secret operator endpoints, keep operational audit append-only, and provision ordinary member permissions through a restricted initializer.

- Add a restricted ordinary-member initializer before retiring legacy operator writes, preserving safe web/backend release ordering.

- Preserve operator tokens through denied-action auditing, align billing directory permissions, reject invalid/cross-origin admin mutations, and correct client-scoped WorkOS verification.

- Harden the standalone operator console: fresh WorkOS authentication, safe local redirects, principal-derived operator permissions, preserved architecture boundaries, and mandatory admin CI.

### Changed

- Migrate the niche UI fills to shadcn internals (Phase 7): the mobile navigation drawer uses Sheet machinery, archive notices use Sonner, workspace and work avatars use Avatar, the Work error banner uses Alert, and UsageMeterBar uses Progress. Canonical Card is exported for later feature adoption. Appearance and public APIs are unchanged.

- **Admin console gets its own Convex secret and operator gate (Stage 2).** New `admin/console:*` functions mirror every admin read/write: each runs `requireAdminSecret` (constant-time `ADMIN_API_SECRET` compare, fails closed when unset) plus `requireOperator` — the caller's WorkOS identity via `convex/auth.config.ts` customJwt providers, plus an active `administrativePrincipals` row with a permitted role. Every write records its own `auditEvents` row in the same transaction, and `admin/breakGlass:grantFirstAdmin` recovers access via `npx convex run` when no admin remains. `apps/admin` now sends `ADMIN_API_SECRET` and the operator's access token; production is unaffected until `ADMIN_API_SECRET` is set (the existing `*ByServer` functions keep serving `/app/admin` on `INTERNAL_API_SECRET`).

- Removed the in-app operator console: `/app/admin`, the `/api/v1/admin/*` routes, `src/features/admin`, the admin sidebar entry, and `AuthorizationAdministrationService` are deleted in favor of the standalone `apps/admin` console (see `internal/plans/ADMIN_CONSOLE_SEPARATION_PLAN.md`). `/app/admin` now returns 404. API-key `admin` scope checks use the ordinary `administration.access` capability, and a `check:admin-separation` CI guard keeps admin routes and `ADMIN_API_SECRET` out of the web build.
- Update Vercel Speed Insights to 2.0 and patch the Agent Host Eve dependency to undici 8.10.2, removing the expired security-audit exception.

- The app boots on one loading screen: the orb and wordmark animate in once, breathe like the chat stream marker, and the "Opening workspace…" line sits beneath them, instead of logo screen to workspace text back to logo. The landing hero gains Get Started and GitHub buttons under the subtitle, and landing logo marks rest on the wordmark's baseline. Reduced motion disables the pulse and workspace spinner; shared pulse timing prevents flashes across loading-boundary handoffs.

- **Sidebar sub-rows share the agent tree's guide-line indent.** Scope sub-rows in the secondary panel (Personal/Workspace's pages such as List/Board/Timeline, All/Notes/Files/Outputs, Connectors/Skills/MCPs, and chat subviews) and folders in the Files tree now nest inside the same `SidebarMenuSub` vertical-line list as an agent's threads, instead of sitting flush or using per-row padding. Behavior and routes are unchanged.

- Upgrade the Redis client to 6.3 and add real TCP regression cases for concurrent rate-limit enforcement and window expiry.

- Require current-head PR QA comments for Builder handoffs, with Vercel MCP browser access, end-to-end results and explicit Integration review/release evidence.

- Use authenticated Vercel MCP access links as the first choice for cloud-agent PR browser QA across platforms, with private runtime automation secrets as a fallback and separate Overlay sandbox login.

- Update CodeQL init, analyze and SARIF upload together to 4.38.2; group future Dependabot updates and enforce matching pins so dependency updates cannot break analysis through version mismatches.

- **Add the standalone admin console app (`apps/admin`).** The operator surface is being split out of the web app per `docs/plans/ADMIN_CONSOLE_SEPARATION_PLAN.md`: Stage 1 scaffolds a separate Next.js app with WorkOS AuthKit sign-in, an `__Host-overlay_admin_session` cookie (8h absolute, 30m idle, recent-sign-in required for destructive actions), a single `convex-admin` server module that calls the existing admin Convex functions with `ADMIN_CONVEX_SECRET`, and the ported Overview/Roles/Groups/Catalog panels. Nothing deploys yet — Vercel project and domain wiring is Stage 3, and `/app/admin` stays in place.

- Restrict preview cleanup to the pinned Overlay project Preview credential and explicitly block the customer production backend, alongside existing project/type/PR ownership checks, to protect production and other products from automation deletion.

- Delete owned Convex Preview backends when a PR closes or loses `needs-preview`; settle builds first, confirm backend absence, and reconcile retained backends so abandoned previews release deployment quota.


- Make PR previews opt-in with `needs-preview`, preserve automatic Git production on main, and clean up previews when the label is removed. Run route/gateway tests once, remove stale test references and tautological/duplicate workflow assertions, and classify ten live-only integration suites separately.
- Run CI static checks, production compilation and exhaustive test shards in parallel; retain required security and merge-queue coverage, avoid duplicate build typechecking and gateway dependency installation, and repair release-document links.
- Resolve first-push Preview PR identity from exact public Git metadata when Vercel starts before the draft PR opens.

- Internal documentation now lives in `internal/` at the repo root (`internal/develop/` living docs, `internal/plans/` point-in-time plans), so `docs/` contains only the public Mintlify site. Public pages must not link to internal docs; `docs:health` was updated accordingly.
- Merged three doc pairs: the computers web-wiring record into `COMPUTERS_PLAN`, the playwright-cli guide into `browser-testing-with-agent-browser.md`, and the connected-agent architecture diagrams into `bring-your-own-agents.md`. Slimmed `traversing-agent-conversations.md` (removed unused Grok Build/Windsurf session formats) and trimmed `todos.md` (removed the completed managed-harness rollout history).
- Added a documentation lifecycle rule to `AGENTS.md`: plans carry a `Status:` line and are deleted by the PR that lands their last phase; dated audits and baselines never live in the repo; no production metrics, customer names, or infra internals in committed docs.
- Consolidate PR previews and native Git production deployments in `overlay-landing`, retaining custom PR URLs, isolated Convex backends and shared Preview test credentials. Retire the separate staging release lane.

### Removed

- Removed 34 internal Markdown files: completed plans and one-time audits (`docs/plans/` history for shipped work — managed harness, workspace scoping, surfaces, sandbox consolidation, work, workspace pricing, agent visibility/creation, BYO agents implementation, release-workflow migration, AI SDK v7, automation durability, complexity and optimization baselines, vision delta), twelve boilerplate or corrupted `packages/*/README.md`, `docs/develop/mcp-servers-overview.md` (folded into `mcp-access.md`), `docs/plans/AGENT_PLAN.md` (folded into `THESIS.md`), and two thin example READMEs (folded into `examples/customer-deployment/README.md`). Living docs in `internal/develop/`, active plans in `internal/plans/`, and all public `.mdx` documentation are unchanged.
- Authorize local laptop agents under DevelopedByDev to integrate directly into main with validated fast-forward pushes; cloud Builder threads retain PR previews and the merge queue.

- Use Vercel’s native Git integration for automatic production deployment from main. Retire the custom production workflow, promotion script and deployment token; Integration verifies production and synchronizes Convex only for backend changes. Staging remains optional and Development Workflow is authoritative.

- **The secondary sidebar uses the shadcn Sidebar.** Agents expand into an indented tree under a vertical guide line (threads, automations, New thread); hovering an agent shows a quick New thread button, and hovering a thread shows Archive and Delete. Loading shows row-shaped placeholders instead of a spinner line. Navigation rows (Chats, Files, Work, Settings) share the same menu parts, and a thin handle on the panel's edge hides it. Behavior and routes are unchanged. Quick creation refreshes the tree and rejects stale bundle responses; hover actions stay scoped to their own row, mobile titles reserve action space, and exported actions do not submit forms by default. New `Sidebar*` parts are exported from `@overlay/ui/primitives`.

- **Work is the home page.** Signing in, opening `/` or `/app`, clicking the Overlay logo, and the marketing site's app links now open Work (`/app/work`) instead of Agents, including password/SSO sign-in without an explicit return destination. Explicit Account/desktop returns are preserved. Agents is unchanged in the sidebar. A deployment with `OVERLAY_FEATURE_WORK=0` shows its "Work unavailable" notice, which now links to Agents.

- **Command search uses cmdk and the shared modal primitives (shadcn Phase 5).** Preserve category drill-in, application-ranked async results, destinations and Overlay themes; keyboard selection, disabled rows, focus containment/return and scroll locking now share canonical owners. Long result descriptions leave their titles visible on mobile.

- **Dialog and form internals use the shadcn primitives (shadcn Phases 1b and 6a).** Remaining centered dialogs and the link-open chooser now share canonical Dialog/Popover machinery, and Button/IconButton/Input/Textarea wrap the canonical implementations while preserving existing exports and appearance. Non-dialog panels and the mobile drawer are unchanged.

- **Selection and status primitives use shadcn internals (shadcn Phase 6b).** Badge now uses variant-backed canonical classes; Toggle wraps the canonical Switch while preserving its checked callback API; Checkbox, Separator, and Skeleton are exported from `@overlay/ui` for later adoption.

- **Tabs and scrolling internals use shadcn primitives (shadcn Phase 6c).** The existing `TabsList`/`TabButton` API now sits on Radix Tabs keyboard semantics, `Toggle` uses canonical Switch machinery, and `ScrollArea`/`ScrollBar` are exported for later surface adoption. Caller-controlled selection stays synchronized with accessible tab state; keyboard focus skips disabled tabs and Enter/Space use existing activation callbacks.

- Keep model details open when opening their nested reasoning select; remove the legacy mobile account outside-click listener so Apps, Usage and Storage disclosures remain open.

- Wrap open-select Tab/Shift+Tab navigation within actual modal dialogs, including disabled footer actions; non-modal popovers advance outside their panel.

- **Selects use Radix with Overlay's existing appearance (shadcn Phase 4).** Shared Select/ListboxSelect now provide keyboard selection/typeahead, collision-aware lists and focus return. Existing generic values, selectable empty options, groups, native form events/reset and import APIs remain intact; dropdown-menu/popover migration stays separate.

### Added

- Phase 2 shadcn migration: conversation action menus gain Radix keyboard navigation/typeahead; account, workspace and model pickers use collision-aware portalled popovers while retaining Overlay themes and selection callbacks. Workspace creation returns focus to its surviving trigger.

- **Work: tasks, board, and timeline (in progress; `OVERLAY_FEATURE_WORK=0` turns it off).** A new Work page above Agents tracks tasks, personal or workspace, as a List, a Board and a Timeline over the same data (`docs/plans/WORK_PLAN.md`). This first part adds the `workItems` and `workItemEvents` tables and their Convex functions: tasks with status, priority, owner, reviewer, dates, a parent, a brief (done-when checklist and context) and an activity log with comments. The HTTP API (`/api/v1/work`, `/work/[id]`, `/work/[id]/move`, `/work/[id]/events`, `/work/context`) and a typed `client.work` ship with it, behind a new `work.use` capability and the `work` feature flag (`OVERLAY_FEATURE_WORK`). The Work page (`/app/work`) sits above Agents in the sidebar, with Personal and Workspace each opening List, Board, and Timeline. The List groups tasks by status (what needs attention first, finished work collapsed), with a one-line quick add, Everything / Needs you / Assigned to me filters, search, and inline status, owner and rename that update at once and roll back with a message if refused. The Board has a column per status: drag a card to another column or to a new place in its own column (Alt and the arrow keys do the same, and each card has a status menu); a refused move snaps back with a message, and the order is kept by the server. The Timeline draws a bar per task across its dates, grouped under the task it sits inside (an objective's bar spans its tasks), with a today line, week headers, and an opening position at today; drag a bar to move it or an end to resize it, or use the arrow keys, and click a task with no dates to schedule it. A task can wait on other tasks (set in its panel, under Waiting on): the Timeline draws an arrow from each blocker to the task that waits on it and the arrows follow bars as they move, and a chain that would loop back on itself is refused. C starts a new task from anywhere on the page, tasks are found from Cmd+K, long lists page by group and the Timeline draws only the rows near the screen, and the page works on a phone (the Board snaps column by column, the Timeline keeps a narrow name column, the task panel covers the screen). The page checks the `work` flag in the browser after the runtime configuration loads, so a preview or deployment with Work on no longer shows "Work unavailable". A browser harness (`npm run test:work:ui`) drives the real UI against an in-page fake API. Opening a task shows its panel: owner, reviewer, priority, dates, parent, a Markdown objective, context (files, notes, chats, links), a done-when checklist, and activity with comments. Tasks can be moved between Personal and Workspace, archived, restored, and deleted for good from Settings → Archived. Personal tasks are private; workspace tasks are shared, can be worked on by members (and always by their assignee), and are moved, archived and restored by the same rules as other content. Review fixes: the shared scope route now honors `work.use` and the Work switch; moving a task releases owners, reviewers, parents and blockers that no longer fit; archived tasks take no comments; long ranks are renumbered on create and status change; task lists read only the view they need; the Timeline shows every level and caps how far it reaches; the page ignores answers to changes made in a scope or workspace you have since left; Work's Convex functions accept only trusted server calls; and a named reviewer can answer a review without edit rights.
- Added a taggable **Development Workflow** guide with Builder and Integration prompts, preview QA, PR handoff, merge ordering, and authorized release steps.
- **Personal plans are gone from the code and the data (workspace pricing, phase 6).** A person's own entitlements are always the free allowance of the workspace they are working in; nothing is ever charged to a person's own account, and only a paid workspace has credit, top-ups and automatic top-ups. The personal subscription writers, the personal branches of the Stripe webhook, the personal balance mirror (`syncPersonalBillingShadows`), personal auto top-ups, the account checkout/portal services, the admin console's personal budget grant (the admin list is now just People), and the old personal billing scripts are deleted; `/api/checkout/verify`, `/api/topups/verify`, `/api/portal` and `POST /api/subscription/settings` answer `410 account_plans_retired`. The plan and credit fields are stripped from people's rows and dropped from the schema. Fixed: in a paid workspace, enabling or running an automation could be refused because the check read the person's (now always free) plan; it now checks the automation's workspace. Memory extraction uses the paid model only when a paid workspace pays for it.
- **Workspace billing has no switch anymore (workspace pricing, phase 6, first part).** The `workspaceWallets` flag, `OVERLAY_FEATURE_WORKSPACE_WALLETS`, `OVERLAY_WORKSPACE_BILLING_ROLLOUT_STAGE` and the internal/selected workspace allowlists are deleted; a workspace with a paid plan always pays, and the Billing tab no longer shows a rollout notice. The plan-conversion tool and its runbook are deleted (the six plans were converted in phase 1); Stripe events for those subscriptions still reach the workspace by subscription id. Behavior in production is unchanged.
- **Accounts are never free or paid; credit expires monthly; plans and top-ups take any amount from $8 to $500 (workspace pricing, phase 4).** Account-plan checkout and account top-ups are retired (`/api/checkout` and `/api/topups/checkout` answer 410); the old account pricing page is deleted; the chat's "add a top-up" opens Workspace → Billing. Usage recorded outside a billing hold (chat suggestions and similar) is now charged to the paid workspace instead of the person's own account. The usage list always adds up to what was charged, with an "Earlier usage" line for charges from before itemization. At each renewal the month's credit and any top-up balance reset; nothing carries over. Workspace → Billing accepts any whole-dollar plan or top-up from $8 to $500.
- **Itemized usage per workspace (workspace pricing, phase 3), and top-ups that settle on their own.** Workspace → Billing has a "Usage this period" list: what the workspace spent on models (expandable per model), Overlay Cloud computers, web search, browser tasks, image and video generation, transcription, memory and knowledge indexing, with quantities, the total, on-demand charges and days left in the cycle. It adds up the charges already recorded for each request; quantities (tokens, minutes) are recorded from now on. `/pricing` now shows the full page, including the example bill; `/pricing/preview` is gone. Automatic top-ups that Stripe finishes later (3-D Secure) are credited as soon as they clear, through new `payment_intent` webhook handling and a 5-minute check with Stripe, and only one runs at a time. Owners and admins get an email once per month when automatic top-ups reach the monthly limit.
- **Paid workspaces choose what happens at $0 (workspace pricing, phase 2).** By default a workspace that runs out of credit stops its paid resources until next month (free ones keep working) and nothing is charged. In Workspace → Billing → When credit runs out, an owner or admin can instead turn on automatic top-ups: pick an amount ($8–$500) and a monthly limit, and Overlay charges the workspace's card for more credit when it runs out, never past that limit in a month; a notice shows when the limit is reached. New `POST /api/v1/workspaces/[workspaceId]/billing/auto-top-up`. `/pricing` now shows the hard stop / keep going choice. Also: the Billing tab no longer shows Overlay's internal provider cost and margin, the plan copy says $8 to $500, the memory profile job no longer fails (and leaves a billing hold) on every run, and memory extraction uses the paid model and bills the workspace when the workspace is paid.
- **Plans belong to workspaces, and the pricing page says so (workspace pricing, phase 1).** Workspace billing is on for every workspace (`OVERLAY_FEATURE_WORKSPACE_WALLETS=true`, stage `general`). A workspace is free or paid; the account has no plan: Settings → Account points to Workspace → Billing instead of showing Free/Paid, upgrade, change plan and top-ups.
